The print is cut to the garment's exact shape, so the neck, shoulders, sleeves and leg openings are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. The outfit's cloth runs unbroken from the collar band across both shoulders and down both sleeves to their hems, so every part of the garment is outfit cloth. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.
